# Worker emergency government (WKR) during the civil war.

focus_tree = {
	id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_focus

	# Each phase has a separate coordinate band and one absolute anchor; other
	# focuses use a prerequisite as relative_position_id. Keep cells unique.
	# Clausewitz routes links through their vertical midpoint: even row gaps
	# need a clear horizontal corridor.
	# Doctrine branches attach to variant settlements. Late-war availability
	# belongs in available so the branch stays visible before the showdown.
	country = {
		factor = 0
		modifier = {
			add = 100
			tag = WKR
		}
	}
	default = no
	continuous_focus_position = { x = 100 y = 555555 }

	# Shared preparation history is copied before this tree is loaded.

	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
	# WKR: constitutional worker federation versus emergency mobilisation.
	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

	focus = {
		id = WKR_affirm_worker_mandate
		icon = GFX_focus_WKR_affirm_worker_mandate
		search_filters = {
			FOCUS_FILTER_MANPOWER
			FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY
			FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL
		}
		x = 8
		y = 7
		cost = 1
		allow_branch = {
			tag = WKR
			OR = {
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_collapse
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_regional_consolidation
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_preparation
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_showdown
			}
		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_focus_phase_tt
				OR = {
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_collapse
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_regional_consolidation
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_preparation
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_showdown
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_if_invalid = yes
		bypass = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_worker_mandate }
		completion_reward = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_safe_with_loyalists }
				unlock_decision_tooltip = WKR_rally_the_chairmans_delegates
			}
			unlock_decision_tooltip = WKR_convene_the_executive_session
			custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_balance_mandate_tt
			add_political_power = 15
			add_stability = 0.02
			add_manpower = 250
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = WRK_birthplace_of_the_first_revolution }
				remove_ideas = WRK_birthplace_of_the_first_revolution
				add_ideas = WRK_birthplace_of_the_first_revolution_front_republic
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_worker_mandate
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { factor = 2 has_stability < 0.50 }
		}
	}

						type = infrastructure
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					owns_state = 201
					controls_state = 201
					201 = { infrastructure < 5 }
				}
				201 = {
					add_building_construction = {
						type = infrastructure
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						AND = {
							owns_state = 200
							controls_state = 200
							200 = { infrastructure < 5 }
						}
					}
					NOT = {
						AND = {
							owns_state = 201
							controls_state = 201
							201 = { infrastructure < 5 }
						}
					}
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = support_equipment
					amount = 60
					producer = WKR
				}

				add_war_support = 0.03

					remove_idea = WKR_lrm_dummy_idea
					add_idea = WKR_lrm_b1_balance_delta_idea
				}
			}
